## <module>  (args=0, locals=0)
   0           0 RESUME                   0

   1           2 LOAD_CONST               0 ("core/recipe_synth.py - turn AI-read ORDER DATA into a VERIFIED, cacheable PoRecipe.\n\nWhy this module exists\n----------------------\nV2.0.0 asked the LLM to *author* the ``line_pattern`` regex. Measured on one real\nLPP PO, 5 independent inferences produced 4 unusable recipes (0 matched rows).\nWriting a character-exact regex blind is simply not something an LLM does\nreliably, and it has no way to test its own answer before replying.\n\nSo the labour is split the way V1.5 split it - and then taken one step further:\n\n    AI    (core/ai/data_extract.py)  -> READS the PO, returns the order DATA.\n    code  (this module)              -> SYNTHESIZES the extraction rule from the\n                                        AI's verbatim sample rows, then REPLAYS\n                                        that rule offline and hard-compares the\n                                        result against the AI's data.\n\nOnly a rule that reproduces the AI's reading exactly is allowed into the\nfingerprint cache. That replay gate is what makes the manual confirmation dialog\nunnecessary: a broken recipe physically cannot be cached, so the next PO of the\nsame layout is parsed offline, zero-AI, with confidence.\n\nPipeline\n--------\n    doc_text ──► AI reads ──► ExtractedData (ground truth)\n                                   │\n                    locate each verbatim row back in doc_text\n                                   │\n              token-align the real rows: constant token -> literal,\n              varying token -> named group  ⇒  line_pattern\n                                   │\n              same trick for the entity/PO anchor and every header field\n                                   │\n                        PoRecipe (candidate)\n                                   │\n                 replay offline: registry.parse(pages, recipe)\n                                   │\n        rows / total qty / per-PO qty / PO-number set must ALL match\n                    ├── match    -> cache it, never call AI for this layout again\n                    └── mismatch -> retry AI once with a concrete hint,\n                                    then raise (never silently export bad data)\n\nNo customer name is hardcoded anywhere: everything comes from the document.\n")
